<!-- pair: MTP-000001 -->

[Initial source blank; electronic chapter marker 1; printed page 417.][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-003]

<!-- pair: MTP-000002 -->

Here is the tantra entitled *The String of Precious Pearls*.[^T01-001][^CH01-G000004][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001]

<!-- pair: MTP-000003 -->

[Printed page 418.][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-004]

<!-- pair: MTP-000004 -->

In the language of India:[^CH01-G000006][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-010]

<!-- pair: MTP-000005 -->

In Tibetan: *The Tantra Entitled The String of Precious Pearls*.[^T01-001][^CH01-G000007][^CH01-G000008][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001]

<!-- pair: MTP-000006 -->

I bow to the Bhagavān, whose very identity is awareness, who has no delusion.[^T01-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-011]

<!-- pair: MTP-000007 -->

At one time when I taught thus:[^T01-003][^CH01-G000010][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001]

<!-- pair: MTP-000008 -->

“Ah! Listen, you great ones whose very identity is delusion! Before my nonexistence, there was awareness free from delusion, called ‘that which has the identity of delusion.’[^T01-002][^T01-004][^CH01-G000014][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-012][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-013][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-014][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-015]

<!-- pair: MTP-000009 -->

[Printed page 419.][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-005]

<!-- pair: MTP-000010 -->

From that—from that not being pure—this great magical display of appearance arose on a vast scale; it dawned in individual objects untainted by delusion.[^T01-005][^T01-016][^CH01-G000016][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-016]

<!-- pair: MTP-000011 -->

At that time, the king that forms appearance arose as many conceptual thoughts of appearance through awareness that distinguishes appearance into classes.[^T01-006][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-017][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-018]

<!-- pair: MTP-000012 -->

I teach [this] as the supreme means of turning awareness back to the Ground in the realm of appearances of delusion. Listen in the three good ways.”[^T01-007][^CH01-G000021][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-019][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-020]

<!-- pair: MTP-000013 -->

[Printed page 420.][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-006]

<!-- pair: MTP-000014 -->

Then the Lord of Secrets, having understood the meaning of what was spoken, said:[^T01-008][^CH01-G000023][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-021]

<!-- pair: MTP-000015 -->

“Thus did I hear at one time. In the sublime palace undivided into parts, in the maṇḍala of the heart (citta),[^T01-003][^T01-009][^CH01-G000026][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-022][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-023][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-024]

<!-- pair: MTP-000016 -->

there were the embodiment of awareness’s sharp vajra, the embodiment of awareness’s vajra chains, the embodiment of awareness’s self-appearance, the embodiment of awareness left as it is, the embodiment of unobstructed awareness, and the embodiment of awareness’s naked appearance. All of these, too, had not moved from the embodiment of the essence of awareness.[^T01-010][^T01-011][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-026][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-027][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-028][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-029][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-030][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-031]

<!-- pair: MTP-000017 -->

There were also an inconceivable retinue of essence—white and red, yellow and green and dark blue, and so forth—and an inconceivable inner retinue of self-appearance.[^T01-011][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-031][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-032][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-033][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-034]

<!-- pair: MTP-000018 -->

[Printed page 421.][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-007]

<!-- pair: MTP-000019 -->

The outer retinue of objects was as follows: the Tathāgata family, the vajra family, the precious-jewel family, the lotus family, and the family free from doing; the wrathful Vajrapāṇi, Hayagrīva, and the Lord of Death of primordial knowing; hosts of ḍākinīs of action, and others beyond measure.[^T01-012][^T01-016][^T01-010][^T01-011][^CH01-G000042][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-034][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-035][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-036][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-037][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-038][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-039][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-040][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-076][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-077]

<!-- pair: MTP-000020 -->

There were the six sages, the retinue that tames appearances in outer objects, and inconceivable emanation embodiments that tame beings.[^T01-011][^T01-013][^T01-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-041][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-042]

<!-- pair: MTP-000021 -->

The retinue of delusion that apprehends was as follows: ignorance, ordinary mind, the mental faculty, the five afflictions,[^T01-014][^T01-011][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-043][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-044][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-045][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-046][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-078]

<!-- pair: MTP-000022 -->

[Printed page 422.][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-008]

<!-- pair: MTP-000023 -->

the apprehending subject, conceptual thought, nonvirtue, and so forth.[^T01-014][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-065][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-066][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-067]

<!-- pair: MTP-000024 -->

Its retinue of appearance was as follows: objects, knowing, mindfulness, that which sees, that which hears, that which smells, that which tastes, that which forms, forms, sounds, smells, tastes, tangible objects, phenomena, and so forth.[^T01-006][^T01-015][^T01-016][^T01-011][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-047][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-048][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-049][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-050][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-051][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-052][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-053][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-054][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-068][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-069][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-070][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-071][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-072][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-073][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-074][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-075][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-079]

<!-- pair: MTP-000025 -->

There were gods, asuras, gandharvas, and an inconceivable retinue of bodhisattvas;[^T01-011][^T01-017][^CH01-G000078][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-054][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-055][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-056]

<!-- pair: MTP-000026 -->

Vinaya, Sūtra, Abhidharma, and so forth;[^T01-017][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-056][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-057][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-058]

<!-- pair: MTP-000027 -->

viewing [things] as a perishing aggregate; hearers, solitary victors, bodhisattvas, Kriyā, and Upa;[^T01-017][^T01-018][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-059][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-060][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-061][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-062][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-063]

<!-- pair: MTP-000028 -->

[Printed page 423.][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-009]

<!-- pair: MTP-000029 -->

Yoga, and, on the inner side, Mahā, Anu, Ati, and so forth. Appearing as retinues of innumerable kinds, they dwelt together.”[^T01-011][^T01-017][^T01-019][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-064]

<!-- pair: MTP-000030 -->

Thus, from the secret tantra *The String of Pearls*: the first chapter, “The Setting.”[^T01-001][^T01-020][^CH01-G000096][^MTP-AUDIT-C01-001]

<!-- pair: MTP-000031 -->

[Electronic chapter marker 2.][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-004]

<!-- pair: MTP-000032 -->

Then the wrathful ones of the three families, the Lord of Secrets and the others, addressed the Bhagavān, the sovereign in whom all delusion is pure, in these words:[^T02-001][^CH02-G000099][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-007][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-008][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-009][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-056]

<!-- pair: MTP-000033 -->

“O, O Bhagavān, embodiment without delusion!
For the sentient beings of saṃsāra in the three realms,
if delusion is reversed, how is it reversed?
Please tell us!”[^T02-001][^T02-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-010][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-011][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-056]

<!-- pair: MTP-000034 -->

Then that Bhagavān Vajrasattva
spoke to the close retinue:[^T02-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-001]

<!-- pair: MTP-000035 -->

“Ah! Listen, Lord of Secrets.
Although there is no delusion in the Ground at the beginning,
there is delusion in sentient beings’ conceptual minds.
If, then, one reaches the meaning of the sap
of awareness’s primordial knowing,[^T02-001][^T02-002][^T02-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-012][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-013][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-014][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-015][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-056]

<!-- pair: MTP-000036 -->

[Printed page 424.][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-005]

<!-- pair: MTP-000037 -->

delusion is reversed in the initial Ground.
Self-awareness’s primordial knowing is difficult to realize.
In the Ground, [there is] an essence endowed with non-conceptuality.
The means of conclusively determining delusion:
spread ignorance and saṃsāra into [unresolved: འདས་ཤེས].[^T02-001][^T02-002][^T02-003][^T02-004][^T02-005][^T02-014][^CH02-G000117][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-019][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-020][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-021][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-022][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-057]

<!-- pair: MTP-000038 -->

Always look at the vajra chains.
Carry whatever appears into the expanse of awareness.
Leave self-awareness at its initial threshold.
Ignorance itself [is cut] at the root;
the appearance of primordial knowing dawns without abiding.[^T02-004][^T02-006][^T02-007][^T02-012][^T02-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-017][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-023][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-026][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-027][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-057]

<!-- pair: MTP-000039 -->

If delusion is reversed, it is reversed in the mother.
The appearance of primordial knowing appears to the child in the intermediate state.[^T02-002][^T02-008][^CH02-G000124][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-018][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-028]

<!-- pair: MTP-000040 -->

Ascertain the root of ordinary mind—the expanse of primordial knowing.
Trace ignorance to its source—the vajra chains.
Cut the flow of the five gates—primordial knowing is clear.
Open the seeing of awareness:
[it] becomes empty appearance.[^T02-006][^T02-009][^T02-015][^T02-020][^T02-025][^CH02-G000128][^CH02-G000129][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-029][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-030][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-031][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-032][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-057]

<!-- pair: MTP-000041 -->

Bring forth the object of delusory appearance—awareness itself.
Cut the flow of mindfulness—the state of the dharma embodiment.
Extract saṃsāra’s quintessence—the three embodiments are clear.[^T02-001][^T02-009][^T02-010][^T02-015][^T02-020][^T02-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-033][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-034][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-035][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-036][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-037][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-058]

<!-- pair: MTP-000042 -->

[Printed page 425.][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-006]

<!-- pair: MTP-000043 -->

Empty and without substance, beautiful with clarity;
sum up the five poisons—the state of medicine.
The cause of darkness exhausted—the lamp itself.
The cause of delusion exhausted—primordial knowing as companion.
The embodiment of clarity blazes—the nondual state.
Insentience and awareness are nondual; saṃsāra is cleared away.
The primordial knowing of meaning returns to the Ground,
of the nonduality of the ultimate and the conventional—[attachment unresolved].[^T02-001][^T02-002][^T02-011][^T02-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-038][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-039][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-040][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-041][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-042][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-043][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-044][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-045][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-058]

<!-- pair: MTP-000044 -->

Ah! Listen, King Hayagrīva.
Bring forth the abiding of ordinary mind—primordial knowing itself.
Conditioned appearance is without an object, all at once.
As for the place of reversal where delusion is reversed:
everything is cleared away in the vajra chains.
In the maṇḍala of the object without mindfulness,
free from differentiating conceptualization, the great continuum is clear.[^T02-001][^T02-002][^T02-006][^T02-010][^T02-012][^T02-016][^T02-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-046][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-047][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-048][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-049][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-050][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-051][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-052][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-058]

<!-- pair: MTP-000045 -->

However many appearances of mindfulness there are,
since they are the cause of great delusion,
in the space where mindfulness is cleared away,
reverse that very delusion.[^T02-002][^T02-010][^T02-020][^T02-021][^T02-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-053][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-054][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-055][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-058][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-074]

<!-- pair: MTP-000046 -->

[Printed page 426.][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-059][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-060]

<!-- pair: MTP-000047 -->

Within the appearance of the object of the nature of phenomena,
however many entities of thinking there are,
since they are the cause of delusion and ignorance,
in awareness without thinking,
reverse that very [delusion] with the eye of meaning.[^T02-002][^T02-013][^T02-017][^T02-018][^T02-020][^T02-021][^T02-022][^T02-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-059][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-075][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-076][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-077][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-079][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-080]

<!-- pair: MTP-000048 -->

Within the appearance of the vajra chains,
whatever conceptual thoughts of the apprehending subject there are,
since those themselves are delusion in actuality,
investigate with discerning knowing that brings realization,
and look at the object of self-appearance.
The root of all phenomena
does not go beyond the two: mindfulness and awareness.[^T02-006][^T02-010][^T02-014][^T02-023][^T02-024][^T02-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-059][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-081][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-082][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-083][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-084]

<!-- pair: MTP-000049 -->

Ah! Listen, you King of the Lord of Death.
Through the light [uncertain: བར་བས] of self-awareness’s primordial knowing,
the very essence of the king of realization—
the embodiment of basic space and vajra chains—
since there is no distraction from that,
is taught as the great realization of continuity.[^T02-001][^T02-004][^T02-006][^T02-009][^T02-014][^T02-015][^T02-020][^T02-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-059][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-067][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-085][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-086][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-087][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-088][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-089][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-090][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-091][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-092]

<!-- pair: MTP-000050 -->

As for the appearances in the conceptual mind that recalls,[^T02-010][^T02-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-059][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-093]

<!-- pair: MTP-000051 -->

[Printed page 427.][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-061][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-062]

<!-- pair: MTP-000052 -->

look at the embodiment of the king of awareness.
Awareness, the unchanging embodiment of primordial knowing,
floats in the self-clear object of the nature of phenomena.
Abiding in the empty object of the nature of phenomena,[^T02-012][^T02-016][^T02-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-061][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-094][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-095][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-096][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-097][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-098][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-099]

<!-- pair: MTP-000053 -->

[Source annotation:] In primordial knowing that is beyond the entity of emptiness.[^T02-013][^T02-017][^T02-018][^T02-022][^T02-025][^CH02-G000178][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-061][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-068]

<!-- pair: MTP-000054 -->

the appearance of embodiment is inconceivable.
In the non-arising nature of phenomena of appearance,
the non-arising nature of phenomena of the three embodiments
is beyond thinking; since it is not apprehended as an entity,
it appears now and arises like the five lights.[^T02-013][^T02-014][^T02-016][^T02-017][^T02-018][^T02-022][^T02-025][^CH02-G000183][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-061][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-069][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-100][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-101][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-102][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-103]

<!-- pair: MTP-000055 -->

‘The appearance of awareness is clear light’[^T02-019][^CH02-G000184][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-061][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-104][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-105]

<!-- pair: MTP-000056 -->

[The source continues with the abbreviation “from the words …”; its intervening scope is unresolved.][^T02-019][^CH02-G000185][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-061][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-070]

<!-- pair: MTP-000057 -->

Various forms of clinging are self-liberated.
The appearance of the dharma embodiment itself
is beyond the phenomena of recalling and thinking.
Since it arises naturally from the basic space of the nature of phenomena,
differentiating conceptualization’s own knot is liberated.
Since doing and the doer do not appear in continuity,[^T02-009][^T02-010][^T02-013][^T02-015][^T02-019][^T02-020][^T02-021][^T02-024][^T02-025][^CH02-G000188][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-061][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-071][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-106][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-107][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-108][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-109][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-110][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-111][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-112][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-113]

<!-- pair: MTP-000058 -->

[Printed page 428.][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-063][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-064]

<!-- pair: MTP-000059 -->

the primordial knowing of delusion, in the mother: [unresolved: ཞ].[^T02-020][^CH02-G000193][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-063][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-072][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-114]

<!-- pair: MTP-000060 -->

Since naturally arising primordial knowing is without a self,
its essence arises as vajra chains.
Self-appearance without an apprehending subject,
not held by an apprehended object, is like the moon in water.
Unobstructed self-liberation
is like a great garuḍa soaring in space.
Without projection or gathering being done, [it is] liberated in its own place.[^T02-006][^T02-014][^T02-020][^T02-021][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-063][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-115][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-116][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-117][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-118]

<!-- pair: MTP-000061 -->

The essence of awareness itself
is liberated in a state without projection or gathering.
The self-appearance of the dharma embodiment
merely appears directly;
it is beyond the mental faculty that recalls entities.
Every appearance as a characteristic, without exception,
attains the non-arising dharma embodiment.[^T02-010][^T02-013][^T02-017][^T02-018][^T02-021][^T02-022][^T02-023][^T02-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-063][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-119][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-120][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-121][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-122][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-123][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-124][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-125][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-126][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-127][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-128]

<!-- pair: MTP-000062 -->

Emaho!
Since awareness’s primordial knowing has no appearance,
the appearance of insentient matter does not obscure me.
Since the empty nature of phenomena has no object,
I do not abide in emptiness.
Since apprehending as characteristics is pure in actuality,
I do not abide in the phenomena of the apprehending subject.[^T02-012][^T02-014][^T02-016][^T02-022][^T02-023][^T02-024][^T02-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-063][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-129][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-130][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-131][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-132][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-133][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-134]

<!-- pair: MTP-000063 -->

[Printed page 429.][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-065][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-066]

<!-- pair: MTP-000064 -->

Since [I] arise naturally through great discerning knowing,
I do not abide in the teachings of the scriptural collections.
Since for me there is neither virtue nor wrongdoing,
I do not abide in the phenomena of saṃsāra.”[^T02-001][^T02-012][^T02-016][^T02-024][^T02-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-065][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-135][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-136][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-137][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-138]

<!-- pair: MTP-000065 -->

Thus, from the secret tantra *The String of Pearls*: the second chapter, “Reversing Delusion from the Ground.”[^T02-001][^T02-002][^CH02-G000220][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-065][^MTP-AUDIT-C02-073]

<!-- pair: MTP-000066 -->

[Electronic chapter marker 3.][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-005]

<!-- pair: MTP-000067 -->

Then Hayagrīva, king of the wrathful ones, petitioned in these words:[^T03-001][^T03-024][^CH03-G000223][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-010][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-011][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-074]

<!-- pair: MTP-000068 -->

“O, O Bhagavān Vajradhara!
The authoritative teaching of self-awareness’s great primordial knowing—
please bestow [it], king of certainty!”[^T03-001][^T03-002][^T03-004][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-012][^T03-020]

<!-- pair: MTP-000069 -->

Then Bhagavān Vajradhara
arose from the seat where he did not abide.
Having uttered the word-formula [retained: སར་བ་འདུས་ཧ],
he spoke to the assembled gathering:[^T03-001][^T03-003][^CH03-G000228][^CH03-G000229][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-009][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-013][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-014][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-015][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-074]

<!-- pair: MTP-000070 -->

“O, O, listen, assembled retinue!
This blazing lamp that makes [things] appear,[^T03-003][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-017][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-018][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-019][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-074][^T03-007]

<!-- pair: MTP-000071 -->

[Printed page 430.][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-006]

<!-- pair: MTP-000072 -->

since [it is] inseparable from the dharma embodiment,
as for the way of activity of the object of self-awareness:
from the embodiment of means, discerning knowing itself—
everything plays in the unceasing expanse.[^T03-004][^T03-005][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-020][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-021][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-022][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-023][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-075][^T03-021]

<!-- pair: MTP-000073 -->

Since secret primordial knowing is unchanging,
the unceasing appearance of intrinsic nature
proliferates like the embodiment of essence itself.
Since [it is] far beyond both doing and the doer,
[it] sees and knows appearance in the object of the nature of phenomena.[^T03-006][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-026][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-027][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-028][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-029][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-075][^T03-021]

<!-- pair: MTP-000074 -->

The sphere of the lamp’s primordial knowing is clear.
In the embodiment of awareness, the nature of phenomena is clear.
[This] is the appearance of the lamp itself.
Then, since [it] opens the way for appearance,
the appearance of awareness is not different.[^T03-007][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-029][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-030][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-031][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-032][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-033][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-034][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-075]

<!-- pair: MTP-000075 -->

This vast universal seal of the secret,
since [it] joins equally with all buddhas—
in limitlessness, no center is an object of focus.[^T03-001][^T03-008][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-034][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-035][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-036][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-037][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-038][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-076]

<!-- pair: MTP-000076 -->

[Printed page 431.][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-007]

<!-- pair: MTP-000077 -->

Since ultimate primordial knowing appears as five,
conventional primordial knowing is varied:
[it] appears like embodiment, and awareness gathers.
Even varied appearances, subtle and coarse,
do not obscure the meaning of primordial knowing.[^T03-009][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-039][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-040][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-041][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-042][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-043][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-044][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-045][^T03-020]

<!-- pair: MTP-000078 -->

Like a butter lamp of a crown jewel,
this light of appearance is immeasurable.
Since the appearance of awareness is clear light,
the realms of the victors of the three embodiments are clear.[^T03-010][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-045][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-046][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-047][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-048][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-049][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-050][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-076]

<!-- pair: MTP-000079 -->

Since the precious pith instruction appears manifestly,
[one] reaches the far shore in the ground of awakening.
[It] is inexpressible, yet appears.[^T03-011][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-051][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-052][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-053][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-054][^T03-020]

<!-- pair: MTP-000080 -->

O, O, Lord of Secrets, holder of the vajra!
If one realizes the meaning of saṃsāra itself,
there is no such thing called ‘nirvāṇa.’
If one realizes the meaning of the five afflictions,[^T03-001][^T03-012][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-004][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-055][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-056][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-057][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-058][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-076][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-077]

<!-- pair: MTP-000081 -->

[Printed page 432.][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-008]

<!-- pair: MTP-000082 -->

primordial knowing does not exist as a name.
If one realizes the meaning of suffering itself,
there is no great bliss to seek.
If one thoroughly realizes the very limit of faults,
there is no need to seek great qualities.
If one realizes the meaning of delusion itself,
there is no need to seek awareness without delusion.[^T03-012][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-004][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-059][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-060][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-061][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-062][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-063][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-064][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-065][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-077][^T03-020]

<!-- pair: MTP-000083 -->

If one realizes the meaning of the five elements themselves,
the five great [ones] are accomplished naturally.
If the root of ignorance itself is ascertained,
the embodiment of awareness itself is accomplished without being accomplished.
If one knows the meaning of sentient beings themselves,
buddhas themselves are found without being sought.
If one realizes the meaning of varied appearances,
the light-appearance of self-awareness is found without being sought.[^T03-001][^T03-004][^T03-013][^T03-014][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-004][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-066][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-067][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-068][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-069][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-070][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-071][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-072][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-073][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-077]

<!-- pair: MTP-000084 -->

If one follows through conceptual thought itself,[^T03-014][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-004][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-088]

<!-- pair: MTP-000085 -->

[Printed page 433.][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-079]

<!-- pair: MTP-000086 -->

that very delusion is liberated in its own place.
If one realizes the meaning of empty appearance,
the embodiment of primordial knowing, too, is spontaneously present.
If one sees the depth of appearing as entities,
emptiness itself, too, is complete without being accomplished.
If one realizes the meaning of A, the nature of phenomena,
[one] reaches the far shore of the non-arising nature of phenomena.[^T03-011][^T03-014][^T03-015][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-089][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-090][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-091][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-092][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-093][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-094][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-095]

<!-- pair: MTP-000087 -->

Emaho!
Self-appearance—the seal of emptiness itself—
is taught as stamped with the seal of knowing.[^T03-015][^T03-016][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-096]

<!-- pair: MTP-000088 -->

Boundless emptiness—the seal of the embodiment of primordial knowing.
Varied ordinary mind itself—the seal of awareness.
The perfectly pure, spacious expanse—the seal of primordial knowing.
Characteristics pure in actuality—the seal of self-awareness.
Appearance complete in itself—the seal of basic space.
Wondrous activity—the seal of appearance.
Wondrous cultivation—the seal of ordinary mind.[^T03-004][^T03-015][^T03-016][^T03-017][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-097][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-098][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-099][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-100][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-101][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-102][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-103]

<!-- pair: MTP-000089 -->

[Printed page 434.][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-080][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-081]

<!-- pair: MTP-000090 -->

Wondrous view—the seal of space.
Wondrous result—the seal of single resolution.
Wondrous primordial knowing—the seal of emptiness.
The wondrous nature of phenomena—the seal of play.
Wondrous realization—the seal of discerning knowing.
Wondrous essence—the seal of vajra chains.
Wondrous authenticity—the seal of awareness.[^T03-016][^T03-017][^T03-018][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-080][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-104][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-105][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-106][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-107][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-108][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-109][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-110]

<!-- pair: MTP-000091 -->

Emaho!
Listen, great Lord of Secrets.
The secret empowerment itself is utterly complete.
This instruction of the core’s vital extract—
in the core itself, the core is stable.[^T03-001][^T03-019][^CH03-G000315][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-080][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-111][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-112][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-113]

<!-- pair: MTP-000092 -->

This pith instruction of ‘the three of certainty’ [grouping unresolved]
becomes the ultimate chief of the tantras.
Since the ultimate and the conventional are nondual,[^T03-020][^T03-024][^CH03-G000316][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-080][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-086][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-114][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-115][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-116][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-117][^T03-002][^T03-009][^T03-011][^T03-021][^T03-022][^T03-023]

<!-- pair: MTP-000093 -->

[Printed page 435.][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-082][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-083]

<!-- pair: MTP-000094 -->

there is great bliss in the object of self-appearance.
The sign that the nature of phenomena is nondual:
[it] does not follow after appearances,
and appears everywhere, yet is nondual.
Not abiding in the phenomena of doing and the doer,
[it] is nondual in the object of self-appearance.[^T03-020][^T03-021][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-082][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-118][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-119][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-120][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-121][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-122][^T03-006][^T03-012][^T03-022]

<!-- pair: MTP-000095 -->

Since [it] does not divide into one and many,
at the time of realization, [it] is nondual.
Since there is no hardship in the means of secret mantra,
at the time of understanding, [it] is nondual.[^T03-021][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-082][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-123][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-124][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-125][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-126][^T03-005][^T03-022]

<!-- pair: MTP-000096 -->

Although [it is] varied in the objects of delusion,
in the ultimate itself there is neither one nor two.
In outer and inner objects there is not even one;
at the time of knowing, variety arises.
Clinging to variety is self-liberated.
Having understood the nature of phenomena, [one is] nondual, and[^T03-022][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-082][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-127][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-128][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-129][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-130][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-131][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-132][^T03-009][^T03-020][^T03-021]

<!-- pair: MTP-000097 -->

[Printed page 436.][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-084][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-085]

<!-- pair: MTP-000098 -->

if the kinds of delusory appearance are traced to their root,
the essence of self-awareness is nondual.
The perfectly pure realm is spontaneously present.”[^T03-004][^T03-014][^T03-015][^T03-022][^T03-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-084][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-133][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-134][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-135][^T03-010][^T03-021]

<!-- pair: MTP-000099 -->

Thus, from the precious secret tantra *The String of Pearls*: the third chapter, “Teaching How to Pass Beyond Delusion.”[^T03-001][^T03-023][^T03-024][^CH03-G000340][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-084][^MTP-AUDIT-C03-087][^T03-020]

<!-- pair: MTP-000100 -->

[Electronic chapter marker 4.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-008]

<!-- pair: MTP-000101 -->

Then Hayagrīva, the king of power, petitioned in these words:[^T04-001][^T04-002][^CH04-G000343][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-019][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-118]

<!-- pair: MTP-000102 -->

“O, O Bhagavān, endowed with compassionate responsiveness!
In accord with our self-awareness,
speak the teaching of the basic space of the nature of phenomena.”[^T04-002][^T04-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-020][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-021]

<!-- pair: MTP-000103 -->

When [he] had thoroughly petitioned in these words,
that Bhagavān, the vajra hero,
arose from deep absorption
and spoke to the nearby retinue:[^T04-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-022][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-023][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-118]

<!-- pair: MTP-000104 -->

“O, O, Hayagrīva, king of power, listen!
I will teach; listen well.[^T04-001][^T04-004][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-001]

<!-- pair: MTP-000105 -->

Because the basic space of phenomena is endowed with adornment,
the embodiment of the primordial knowing of all buddhas—
of all buddhas and sentient beings, [retained: དུར]
[retained: ཐེམ་ཞིང]; [it] acts in the authentic meaning.[^T04-002][^T04-005][^CH04-G000355][^CH04-G000356][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-015][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-026][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-118]

<!-- pair: MTP-000106 -->

The non-arising appearance of awareness[^T04-006][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-027]

<!-- pair: MTP-000107 -->

[Printed page 437.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-009]

<!-- pair: MTP-000108 -->

is beyond both what arises and what brings about arising.
The secret embodiment and the state of awareness,
in the completely pure nature of phenomena,
are shaped by compassionate responsiveness and cannot be cut off.[^T04-002][^T04-006][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-028][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-029][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-030][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-031][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-119]

<!-- pair: MTP-000109 -->

Excellent adornment of the basic space of the nature of phenomena:
the appearance of spheres is boundless;
the essence of embodiment—vajra chains;
the five light rays of the appearance of primordial knowing—
the embodiment of the path of all buddhas.”[^T04-002][^T04-004][^T04-007][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-032][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-033][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-034][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-035][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-036][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-119]

<!-- pair: MTP-000110 -->

In the vast mandala of the nature of phenomena,
the activity of the means of secret mantra
was taught by Bhagavān Vajrasattva:[^T04-001][^T04-002][^T04-004][^T04-008][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-037][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-038]

<!-- pair: MTP-000111 -->

“Emaho!
Great appearance has great clarity.
Pure primordial knowing is clear as light.
The expanse of the nature of phenomena, without conceptual elaborations—
this spontaneously vast, unchanging palace—
since [it] appears individually and serves purposes,[^T04-004][^T04-008][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-039][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-040][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-041][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-042][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-043][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-119][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-120]

<!-- pair: MTP-000112 -->

[Printed page 438.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-010]

<!-- pair: MTP-000113 -->

the expanse of the embodiment of self-awareness’s primordial knowing
plays in the space of empty primordial knowing.
Authentic primordial knowing is unceasing and vast.
The primordial knowing of deep absorption is like fire.
Authentic primordial knowing is like wind.
The means of unmoved primordial knowing are secret, and
the nature of phenomena is an excellent adornment of the body.[^T04-002][^T04-008][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-044][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-045][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-046][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-047][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-048][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-049]

<!-- pair: MTP-000114 -->

The activity of the dharma embodiment
is the activity of all buddhas; and
the abiding place of the spheres
is naturally unmoved, without movement.[^T04-009][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-050][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-051][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-052][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-053][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-120]

<!-- pair: MTP-000115 -->

Since the primordial knowing of the view is skilled in means,
in the state of unmoved Vajrasattva,
since the embodiment of varied play is shown,
[it] appears continuously and fully plays.
Since the means of secret mantra have many gates,
[it] is thoroughly taught through the three assurances.[^T04-001][^T04-002][^T04-010][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-004][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-054][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-055][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-056][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-057][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-058][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-059][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-120]

<!-- pair: MTP-000116 -->

[Printed page 439.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-011]

<!-- pair: MTP-000117 -->

The root of all phenomena
is liberated in its own place without enacting activity.
Those that appear like embodiment through means,
abiding on the path of secret mantra,
are beyond thinking, utterly pure, and beyond objects.[^T04-002][^T04-011][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-004][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-060][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-061][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-062][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-063][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-064][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-121]

<!-- pair: MTP-000118 -->

The objects of habitual tendencies are the state of embodiment.
All secret mantra comes down to means.
Primordial knowing without attachment—the state of speech—
is taught as the view of great spontaneous completeness.
[It] joins equally with all buddhas,
compassionate responsiveness, with stains completely pure,
becoming great discerning knowing.[^T04-002][^T04-012][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-004][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-065][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-066][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-067][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-068][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-069][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-121]

<!-- pair: MTP-000119 -->

Naturally arising primordial knowing, empty and pure—
the root of all phenomena—
[is] the state of secret mantra’s vajra chains.
The natural arising of all phenomena
[is] the lamp of basic space, the seal of means.[^T04-002][^T04-013][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-004][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-005][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-070][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-071][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-072][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-073][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-074][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-121]

<!-- pair: MTP-000120 -->

[Printed page 440.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-012]

<!-- pair: MTP-000121 -->

The dharma embodiment appears on the path of entities.
Primordial knowing without stains
strikes the vital point of the object of emptiness.
The king of pith instruction binds all together, and
everything—self-appearance—is spontaneously complete.[^T04-002][^T04-013][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-005][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-075][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-076][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-077][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-079][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-122]

<!-- pair: MTP-000122 -->

Emaho!
As for the view of all yogas:
the gate of ordinary mind is closed; differentiating conceptualization clears.
The pith instruction: a spacious house, a collapsed interior;
with desire’s gate closed, primordial knowing is clear within.[^T04-002][^T04-014][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-005][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-080][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-081][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-082][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-083][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-122]

<!-- pair: MTP-000123 -->

If [one] carries the nature of phenomena onto the path, primordial knowing’s appearances—
dogs and thieves are brought out [མདོར: relation unresolved]; the essence of awareness is held.
Marks are subdued; appearance is stamped with a seal.
Primordial knowing—unchanging, a vast expanse—fully masters light’s basic space.
Discerning knowing—self-appearance—is ascertained in the object of the nature of phenomena.[^T04-002][^T04-015][^T04-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-005][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-006][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-084][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-085][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-086][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-087][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-088][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-089][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-122]

<!-- pair: MTP-000124 -->

[Printed page 441.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-013]

<!-- pair: MTP-000125 -->

The nature of phenomena, free from conditions, abides as the core of all.
[With] appearance resolved in one [point], run into space.[^T04-002][^T04-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-006][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-090][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-091][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-123]

<!-- pair: MTP-000126 -->

Emaho!
Rest self-awareness’s primordial knowing in an unmoved state.
Carry ignorance itself into the expanse.
Turn back even saṃsāra [རུ་ནས: locus unresolved].
[It] is not distinguished by activity and does not exist as an object.[^T04-002][^T04-017][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-006][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-092][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-093][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-094][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-095]

<!-- pair: MTP-000127 -->

The five roots themselves are the embodiment of primordial knowing.
Since that which remembers is cleared away, pure self-awareness
appears distinctly on the path of all.[^T04-002][^T04-018][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-006][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-096][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-097][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-098][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-123]

<!-- pair: MTP-000128 -->

In the mirror of the all-basis body,
since the ground of empty, clear awareness is shown,
delusion arising from another is brought to the ground.
Since the five aggregates themselves are without cause,
the body of delusion is manifestly buddha.[^T04-002][^T04-019][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-006][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-007][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-099][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-100][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-101][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-102][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-103][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-123]

<!-- pair: MTP-000129 -->

[Printed page 442.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-014]

<!-- pair: MTP-000130 -->

Since the five elements themselves are primordially clarified,
appearance is liberated into clear-light sleep.
Since the five faculties themselves are left in naked resting,
the five objects of the apprehending subject are liberated in their own place.
Since the five afflictions themselves are [one’s] own,
the three realms themselves are brought to the ground.[^T04-002][^T04-020][^CH04-G000446][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-007][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-017][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-104][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-105][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-106][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-107][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-108][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-109][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-124]

<!-- pair: MTP-000131 -->

Since ignorance itself arises naturally,
the very cause of delusion is cleared away from the beginning.
Since the all-basis self-arises as the dharma embodiment,
the arising of the continuum is cut at the root.
Since [it is] from the beginning in wish-fulfilling enlightened intent [construction unresolved],
those that bring about movement are self-resting.[^T04-002][^T04-021][^CH04-G000456][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-007][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-018][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-110][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-111][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-112][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-113][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-114][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-115][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-124]

<!-- pair: MTP-000132 -->

Since the continuity of breath has been cut from the beginning,
birth and death have not existed from the very beginning.[^T04-022][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-007][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-116][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-117][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-143]

<!-- pair: MTP-000133 -->

[Printed page 443.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-125][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-127]

<!-- pair: MTP-000134 -->

Since the qualities of desire are primordially complete,
the limit of clinging is cultivation itself.
Since [one] has primordially arrived without moving the limbs,
the path of going has been traversed from the beginning.
Since spoken speech is inexpressible,
[it] is beyond the objects of speech and thinking from the beginning.[^T04-002][^T04-022][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-126][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-144][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-145][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-146][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-147][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-148]

<!-- pair: MTP-000135 -->

Since the proliferating and gathering of nonexistence are primordially empty,
[one] abides in great meditative concentration from the beginning.
Since the stains are self-purified,
[one] abides in great stainless unimpeded penetration.
Because what is done has not arisen,
[one is] free from doing and the doer from the outset.[^T04-002][^T04-023][^CH04-G000469][^CH04-G000470][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-126][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-149][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-150][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-151][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-152][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-153]

<!-- pair: MTP-000136 -->

Since, from the ground, no phenomena arise from another,[^T04-002][^T04-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-126][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-154][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-155]

<!-- pair: MTP-000137 -->

[Printed page 444.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-128][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-130]

<!-- pair: MTP-000138 -->

from the very beginning, [there is] only natural arising.
Since the numbers one and two are exhausted,
the sphere itself is without a companion.
Since darkness is completely pure from the very beginning,
the appearance of awareness pervades as clear light.[^T04-002][^T04-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-129][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-156][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-157][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-158][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-159][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-160]

<!-- pair: MTP-000139 -->

Since saṃsāra, without cause, has already ceased,
[there is] the level of buddha itself from the beginning.
Since entities of marks are emptied and cleared away,
the self-grasping conceptual mind is exhausted from the beginning.
Since conditions are self-liberated by conditions,
dependent phenomena are left as they are.”[^T04-002][^T04-004][^T04-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-129][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-161][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-162][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-163][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-164][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-165]

<!-- pair: MTP-000140 -->

Then King Hayagrīva himself,
since all those appearances were self-liberated,[^T04-101][^T04-102][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-129][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-166]

<!-- pair: MTP-000141 -->

[Printed page 445.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-131][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-133]

<!-- pair: MTP-000142 -->

having become supremely astonished,
fainted and fell hard to the ground.[^T04-102][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-132][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-167]

<!-- pair: MTP-000143 -->

Then that great Vajradhara,
in order to raise him from his faint in that state,
expressly uttered these words:[^T04-101][^T04-102][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-132][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-168][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-169][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-170]

<!-- pair: MTP-000144 -->

“Ema! If appearances such as those,
even delusion itself, are self-liberated,
why are appearances free from delusion not liberated?
Whatever the speaking, and all doing and doers,
are the activity of clear empty awareness.[^T04-103][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-132][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-171][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-172][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-173][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-174]

<!-- pair: MTP-000145 -->

Conceptual thoughts of good and bad
are cultivation’s great river-expanse.
Wrong views and desires for what is authentic
are yoga’s impartial view.
All apprehending in hope and fear[^T04-104][^T04-105][^T04-106][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-132][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-175][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-176][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-177][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-178][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-179][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-223]

<!-- pair: MTP-000146 -->

[Printed page 446.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-134][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-136]

<!-- pair: MTP-000147 -->

is the result of pure penetration arising.[^T04-106][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-135][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-180]

<!-- pair: MTP-000148 -->

With the desire for Mahamudra to be manifest,
in the mandala of the entire three-thousandfold [world],
rain and liquid vapor are sprinkled.
The path of going, as an object of focus, is the great line;
footprints are the pattern of colored powder.
The wish to go is the posture itself;
moving the limbs is the gesture.[^T04-107][^T04-108][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-135][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-181][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-182][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-183][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-184][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-185][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-186]

<!-- pair: MTP-000149 -->

Whatever is spoken is a word of mantra.
Acts of thinking are the generation stage.
Movement in the mental faculty is offering itself.
Appearance as form is the deity’s embodiment.
The utterance of loud sound is music itself.[^T04-108][^T04-109][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-135][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-187][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-188][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-189][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-190][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-191][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-192]

<!-- pair: MTP-000150 -->

[Printed page 447.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-137][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-139]

<!-- pair: MTP-000151 -->

One’s own body is the vase;
hair is the foliage, the beautiful adornment at its mouth.
Blood and serum are the waters themselves.
[It is] filled with substances—the vital organs, heart, and so forth.
The empowerments appearing to oneself are bestowed;
without being conferred, [they] are complete.[^T04-110][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-138][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-193][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-194][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-195][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-196][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-197][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-198]

<!-- pair: MTP-000152 -->

The desire to transgress is samaya;
the desire to guard is bondage itself.
The desire to liberate is deterioration;
the desire for absence is the supreme sage.[^T04-111][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-138][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-199][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-200]

<!-- pair: MTP-000153 -->

Eating and drinking are approach;
lying down and sitting are accomplishment.
Formations are consecration itself.
With the lungs and [retained: ལུང་པ], the mandala is accomplished.
Things seen are signs of the gesture;
attachment to those is experiential acquaintance.[^T04-108][^T04-112][^T04-113][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-138][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-201][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-202][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-203][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-204][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-205][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-206][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-207][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-224]

<!-- pair: MTP-000154 -->

[Printed page 448.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-140][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-142]

<!-- pair: MTP-000155 -->

Clear knowing-awareness is instruction;
object and ordinary mind gathered together are the object of application.
Birth, aging, sickness, and death are the vital point of familiarization.
The unceasing six collections are realization.[^T04-114][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-141][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-208][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-209][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-210][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-211]

<!-- pair: MTP-000156 -->

Then the primordial-knowing ḍākinīs,
being intensely joyful, delighted, and rejoicing,
petitioned Vajradhara in these words:[^T04-101][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-141][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-212][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-213][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-214]

<!-- pair: MTP-000157 -->

“O! If liberation free from delusion is like that,
why is the container-world not liberated?”[^T04-115][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-141][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-215][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-216]

<!-- pair: MTP-000158 -->

Vajradhara spoke:[^T04-101][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-141][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-217]

<!-- pair: MTP-000159 -->

“Since space itself is pervasive and empty,
space itself is liberated as an entity.
Since wind appears without an apprehended object,
[it] is liberated as the work of gathering entities.
Since fire is the ripener, the eater of offerings,[^T04-116][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-141][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-218][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-219][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-220][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-221][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-222][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-239]

<!-- pair: MTP-000160 -->

[Printed page 449.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-225][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-226]

<!-- pair: MTP-000161 -->

[it] is liberated as moving and separating the refined portion and dregs.
Since earth generates and supports,
[it] is liberated as insubstantial, limitless emptiness.
Since water gathers and moistens,
[it] is liberated as the work of burning and ripening.[^T04-116][^T04-117][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-225][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-240][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-241][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-242][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-243]

<!-- pair: MTP-000162 -->

The wind of space is an entity;
the fire of space gathers.
The water of space burns.
The earth of space opens room.
The space of space is appearance.[^T04-118][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-225][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-244][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-245][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-246][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-247][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-248]

<!-- pair: MTP-000163 -->

The wind of wind is stable;
the space of wind has great primordial radiance.
The water of wind is swift;
the fire of wind divides projection and gathering.
Through wind, as for earth, [its] reflection is clear.[^T04-118][^T04-119][^T04-120][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-225][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-249][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-250][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-251][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-252][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-253]

<!-- pair: MTP-000164 -->

[Printed page 450.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-227][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-228]

<!-- pair: MTP-000165 -->

The water of water is melodious sound.
The space of water is unobstructed.
The wind of water flows without attachment.
The fire of water gathers.
Water pervades earth.[^T04-109][^T04-118][^T04-120][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-227][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-254][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-255][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-256][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-257][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-258]

<!-- pair: MTP-000166 -->

The fire of fire generates.
The space of fire burns entities.
The wind of fire makes [them] blaze.
The water of fire destroys wood.
The earth of fire holds.[^T04-118][^T04-121][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-227][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-259][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-260][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-261][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-262][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-263]

<!-- pair: MTP-000167 -->

The earth of earth pervades beings.
The space of earth is the great vehicle.
The wind of earth fills and empties.
The water of earth generates entities.
The fire of earth dries.[^T04-118][^T04-122][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-227][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-264][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-265][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-266][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-267][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-268]

<!-- pair: MTP-000168 -->

Through arising without determination,[^T04-123][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-227][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-237][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-269][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-270]

<!-- pair: MTP-000169 -->

[Printed page 451.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-229][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-230]

<!-- pair: MTP-000170 -->

ordinary mind itself, appearing definitely, is liberated.
All appearances that do not exist as form
are liberated in the state without intrinsic nature.
The formless are liberated by entities.
Phenomena are liberated by phenomena;
even absence is liberated by absence.
One is liberated by one;
through appearing as two, the two are liberated.”[^T04-123][^T04-124][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-229][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-271][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-272][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-273][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-274][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-275][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-276][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-277][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-278]

<!-- pair: MTP-000171 -->

Glorious Supreme Horse again petitioned:[^T04-101][^T04-125][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-229]

<!-- pair: MTP-000172 -->

“O Bhagavān Vajradhara!
Since even the container-world has become liberated,
why are its contents, sentient beings, not liberated?
For liberated sentient beings, [this] would become meaningless.
Or are all those liberated ones[^T04-101][^T04-115][^T04-126][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-229][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-279][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-280][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-281][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-282][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-283][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-284][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-285][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-286][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-287][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-288][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-289]

<!-- pair: MTP-000173 -->

[Printed page 452.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-231][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-232]

<!-- pair: MTP-000174 -->

liberated without effort—or what?
If liberation is through effort, and the inert
are not reasonably to be liberated,
as for the liberation of sentient beings, how are they liberated?”[^T04-126][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-231][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-290][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-291][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-292][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-293][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-294]

<!-- pair: MTP-000175 -->

The Bhagavān spoke:[^T04-101][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-231][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-295][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-296]

<!-- pair: MTP-000176 -->

“Liberation is not through effort;
from the very primordial beginning, [one] abides liberated.[^T04-127][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-231][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-297][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-298]

<!-- pair: MTP-000177 -->

From the joining of means and discerning knowing,
the very causes of father and mother,
moved by the propelling wind,
[are] awakening, self-awareness, great bliss.[^T04-128][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-231][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-299][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-300][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-301][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-302][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-303][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-304]

<!-- pair: MTP-000178 -->

From the causes of the five elements, the seed
arises distinctly from the object of emptiness.
The bliss of the two, their absorption,
is discerning knowing arisen from means.[^T04-128][^T04-129][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-231][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-305][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-306][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-307][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-308]

<!-- pair: MTP-000179 -->

[Printed page 453.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-233][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-234]

<!-- pair: MTP-000180 -->

Entry into the enclosure of the womb
is self-awareness arising as appearance from the ground.
[It is] the measure of realization of seven groups of seven.
Ten months—traversing the levels themselves.[^T04-130][^T04-135][^CH04-G000618][^CH04-G000619][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-233][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-309][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-310][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-311][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-312]

<!-- pair: MTP-000181 -->

Birth itself is arising as embodiment.
The body’s growth is the object of ground-appearance.
Abiding in the body is the ground.
Aging is delusion clearing away.
Sickness itself is the confidence of realization.
Through death, [one] is liberated into the empty nature of phenomena.[^T04-131][^T04-135][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-233][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-313][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-314][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-315][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-316][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-317][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-318]

<!-- pair: MTP-000182 -->

Thus, sentient beings in form
are primordially liberated without effort.
Ema! Activity, thinking, transformation—appearance is liberated.
Uncontrived by cultivation, the bearer of phenomena is liberated.[^T04-132][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-233][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-319][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-320][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-321][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-322][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-323]

<!-- pair: MTP-000183 -->

[Printed page 454.][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-235][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-236]

<!-- pair: MTP-000184 -->

Unexamined by view, permanence and annihilation are liberated.
Unbound by the result, one’s own body is liberated.
Appearance is stamped with the primordial seal.”[^T04-133][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-235][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-324][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-325][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-326]

<!-- pair: MTP-000185 -->

Thus, from the Precious String of Pearls Tantra, this is the fourth chapter, on effortless self-liberation.[^T04-134][^CH04-G000634][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-235][^MTP-AUDIT-C04-238]

<!-- endnotes -->

[^T01-001]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 417–418, 423, MTP-S000004, MTP-S000008, MTP-S000096. Tibetan: རྒྱུད. The canonical assignment is “continuum.” These occurrences name the present text, two in title formulas with ཅེས་བྱ་བ and one in the chapter colophon. “Tantra” is an occurrence-specific, provisional text-genre rendering; the canonical entry remains unchanged, and this choice does not govern other uses of རྒྱུད. Review action: approve or replace this title/colophon usage. The full opening title retains “precious,” while the shorter colophon title does not add it.

[^T01-002]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 418–419, MTP-S000009, MTP-S000011, MTP-S000014. Tibetan: བཅོམ་ལྡན་འདས; རིག་པའི་བདག་ཉིད; འཁྲུལ་པའི་བདག་ཉིད་ཆེན་པོ་རྣམས; འཁྲུལ་པའི་བདག་ཉིད་ཅན. The honorific is represented by the conventional name Bhagavān, provisionally, without claiming a Sanskrit source reading. The unlisted བདག་ཉིད is provisionally “very identity” or “identity”; ཅན is retained as “has.” This avoids silently assigning the reserved “essence” (ངོ་བོ) or “embodiment” (སྐུ) to a different source expression. The relation may instead concern personification or essential character; the source alone does not settle a universal English equivalent. Review these three constructions together; do not infer a permanent mapping.

[^T01-003]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 418, 420, MTP-S000010, MTP-S000024. Tibetan: འདི་སྐད་བདག་གིས་བསྟན་པའི་དུས་གཅིག་ན / འདི་སྐད་བདག་གིས་ཐོས་པའི་དུས་གཅིག་ན. The first formula has བསྟན, “taught,” whereas the second has ཐོས, “heard.” The English preserves the first-person agent in both and does not regularize the first to the familiar hearing formula. The temporal construction is translated provisionally as “at one time when I taught” in the first formula and “Thus did I hear at one time” in the second. Review the temporal attachment and quotation punctuation; no source verb has been changed.

[^T01-004]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 418–419, MTP-S000012, MTP-S000013, MTP-S000014. Tibetan: ང་མེད་པའི་སྔོན་རོལ་ན / འཁྲུལ་པ་མེད་པའི་རིག་པ / འཁྲུལ་པའི་བདག་ཉིད་ཅན་ཞེས་བྱ་བ་ཡོད་དེ. The overt negation in ང་མེད་པའི་སྔོན་རོལ་ན qualifies the phrase governed by “before.” The revised draft therefore keeps the literal, temporally difficult “Before my nonexistence.” The earlier “Before I was” hid or reversed that scope and is preserved only in revisions/translation-draft-01.md. Neither a prior-existence narrative nor a philosophical no-self reading is established; the temporal relation remains unresolved. The next clauses call awareness free from delusion “that which has the identity of delusion.” This apparent tension is retained; neither a negation nor a different speaker is supplied. Review the relation of the name to awareness and the temporal phrase against the full source context. The working syntax is provisional, not an emendation.

[^T01-005]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 419, MTP-S000016, MTP-S000017. Tibetan: དེ་ལས་དེ་མ་དག་པ་ལས་འདི་ལྟར་སྣང་བའི་ཆོ་འཕྲུལ་ཆེན་པོ་ཆེར་བྱུང་སྟེ / འཁྲུལ་པས་མ་བགོས་པའི་ཡུལ་སོ་སོར་ཤར་རོ. The repeated དེ and two ལས constructions leave reference and causal attachment difficult. The draft provisionally reads “from that—from that not being pure”; it preserves མ་དག་པ and does not make the cause pure. The following “it” refers provisionally to the magical display; “in individual objects” renders ཡུལ་སོ་སོར. That locative relation, rather than “as individual objects,” requires review. The negative “untainted by delusion” is preserved despite the preceding impurity. No doctrinal explanation resolves the tension in the root translation.

[^T01-006]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 419, 422, MTP-S000018, MTP-S000019, MTP-S000070. Tibetan: དེའི་ཚེ་སྣང་བ་འདུ་བྱེད་ཀྱི་རྒྱལ་པོ / སྣང་བ་རིས་སུ་འབྱེད་པའི་རིག་པས་སྣང་བའི་རྟོག་པ་དུ་མ་ལྟར་བྱུང་ངོ / འདུ་བྱེད་དང. The unlisted འདུ་བྱེད is provisionally rendered with “form”: “the king that forms appearance” and “that which forms.” In the first construction the draft treats the king as the subject of བྱུང and རིག་པས as instrumental; “appearance, king of formations” remains a possible different attachment. In the faculty list the draft does not silently replace the word with a missing tactile faculty or identify it with ordinary mind, mental faculty, consciousness, or contrivance. Review both constructions before fixing a technical default. The adjacent “forms” in English represents the distinct གཟུགས, not a second occurrence of འདུ་བྱེད.

[^T01-007]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 419–420, MTP-S000020, MTP-S000021. Tibetan: འཁྲུལ་པའི་སྣང་བ་ཞིང་ཁམས་སུ་རིག་པ་གཞི་ལ་བཟློག་པའི་ཐབས་མཆོག་ཏུ་ངས་བསྟན་གྱིས / ལེགས་པའི་ཚུལ་གསུམ་གྱིས་ཉོན་ཅིག. The draft takes ཞིང་ཁམས་སུ locatively, “in the realm,” and relates that realm to the preceding appearances of delusion. An alternative resultative attachment (“as a realm”) remains open; neither a realm of a buddha nor a conversion of appearances into such a realm is added as established meaning. “[This]” supplies an English object of “teach,” without identifying an unstated referent. “The three good ways” preserves the number but does not supply a doctrinal list. For གཞི the lexical assignment “The Ground” is retained; lower-case article with internal capital (“the Ground”) is a consistent provisional presentation choice. Review scope, the realm construction, the threefold instruction, and capitalization separately.

[^T01-008]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 420, MTP-S000023. Tibetan: གསང་བའི་བདག་པོས. “The Lord of Secrets” provisionally translates the unlisted epithet. The ergative marking identifies this figure as the one who understands and speaks. The translation does not add an identification with another named figure; such an identification requires separate evidence. Review the epithet as a proposed whole expression, not as a new equivalent for བདག་པོ in every context.

[^T01-009]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 420, MTP-S000025, MTP-S000026. Tibetan: ཆ་ཡིས་མ་བགོས་པའི་གཞལ་ཡས་ཁང་དམ་པ / ཐུགས་ཙིཏྟའི་དཀྱིལ་འཁོར་ན. The draft provisionally renders the unlisted ཐུགས as “heart” in ཐུགས་ཙིཏྟའི་དཀྱིལ་འཁོར, and དཀྱིལ་འཁོར as “maṇḍala.” “Heart” remains uncertain beside a mind-related reading; it is not the glossary’s “ordinary mind,” “conceptual mind,” “mental faculty,” or “enlightened intent.” “Citta” is a provisional conventional Roman representation of the supplied Tibetan Sanskrit term, not a reconstructed Sanskrit title. The two locations are left in sequence, without adding a physiological location or deciding that palace and maṇḍala are separate places. Review the heart/mind relation and the locative attachment. The exact Tibetan remains unchanged.

[^T01-010]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 420–421, MTP-S000027, MTP-S000028, MTP-S000029, MTP-S000030, MTP-S000031, MTP-S000032, MTP-S000033, MTP-S000041. Tibetan: རིག་པ་རྡོ་རྗེ་ལུ་གུ་རྒྱུད་ཀྱི་སྐུ; རིག་པ་ཅོག་བཞག་གི་སྐུ; རིག་པ་ཐོགས་པ་མེད་པའི་སྐུ; རིག་པ་གཅེར་སྣང་གི་སྐུ. All six embodiments retain “awareness” and “embodiment.” Standalone རྡོ་རྗེ is provisionally “vajra” in the sharp-vajra embodiment and the vajra family; the existing “Eleven vajra topics” entry supports this component but does not supply a standalone default. In the second, the full རྡོ་རྗེ་ལུ་གུ་རྒྱུད is provisionally represented once by “vajra chains”: the source’s explicit རྡོ་རྗེ and the vajra component already present in the canonical ལུ་གུ་རྒྱུད assignment overlap. This whole-expression treatment requires review; it does not establish a new standalone meaning of ལུ་གུ་རྒྱུད. The source spelling ཅོག་བཞག differs from glossary ཅོག་གཞག (“leaving as it is”); “awareness left as it is” is a proposed form-linked grammatical realization, not a source correction. “Unobstructed” and “naked appearance” are provisional gaps; naked appearance is not silently changed to the distinct “naked seeing” or “naked resting.” Review the nominal attachments and these usages together.

[^T01-011]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 420–423, MTP-S000027, MTP-S000033–MTP-S000034, MTP-S000037, MTP-S000039, MTP-S000050, MTP-S000053, MTP-S000062, MTP-S000077, MTP-S000080, MTP-S000094–MTP-S000095. Tibetan anchors 27–94 form an extended enumeration ending in ཐབས་ཅིག་ཏུ་བཞུགས་སོ (95). The draft supplies English “there were” in several list units, anticipating the final “dwelt together,” and divides the long construction into readable sentences. This is grammatical presentation, not an assertion of creation or of distinct chronological events. The recurring unlisted འཁོར is provisionally “retinue” in these assembly and relational lists; this does not establish “retinue” as its universal meaning. “All of these, too, had not moved” preserves the negation and attachment to the embodiment of the essence of awareness in anchor 33. At the end, “they” resumes the assembled retinues. The clause སྣ་ཚོགས་སུ་སྣང་བ preserves appearing-as, rather than simply being many entities. Review the scope of the final residence verb and the color/retinue attachment; no enumeration members are rearranged.

[^T01-012]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 421, MTP-S000039, MTP-S000040, MTP-S000041, MTP-S000042, MTP-S000043, MTP-S000044, MTP-S000045, MTP-S000046, MTP-S000047, MTP-S000048, MTP-S000049. Tibetan: རིགས; དེ་བཞིན་གཤེགས་པ; བྱ་བ་དང་བྲལ; ཁྲོ་བོ་ཕྱག་ན་རྡོ་རྗེ; རྟ་མགྲིན; ཡེ་ཤེས་ཀྱི་གཤིན་རྗེ; ལས་ཀྱི་མཁའ་འགྲོ་མའི་ཚོགས. The unlisted group term is provisionally “family”; the names Tathāgata, Vajrapāṇi, Hayagrīva and ḍākinī are conventional target-language representations, not recovered Sanskrit spellings from this Tibetan page. The fifth family retains the actual “free from doing,” preserving the glossary’s doing/freedom relation rather than substituting a conventional family label. “Lord of Death of primordial knowing” preserves the full assigned knowing expression. “Ḍākinīs of action” retains ལས as a separate unresolved technical use; it is not silently identified with the different བྱ་བ (“doing”) or with ལས་ཀྱི་རླུང (“karmic wind”). “Wrathful” explicitly modifies Vajrapāṇi; its possible wider scope is not asserted. Review the names, family terminology and scope before approving these whole-expression usages.

[^T01-013]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 421, MTP-S000050, MTP-S000051, MTP-S000052. Tibetan: ཕྱི་ཡུལ་ལ་སྣང་བ་འདུལ་བའི་འཁོར་ཐུབ་པ་དྲུག་དང / འགྲོ་བ་འདུལ་བའི་སྤྲུལ་པའི་སྐུ / བསམ་གྱིས་མི་ཁྱབ་པ་དང. “Six sages” provisionally renders ཐུབ་པ་དྲུག; no six names or realms are inserted. The draft keeps སྣང་བ as the expressed complement of taming in anchor 50, hence “tames appearances in outer objects.” The relation of ཕྱི་ཡུལ་ལ to appearing and taming needs review; “tames beings” is explicit only in the following འགྲོ་བ་འདུལ་བ phrase. The canonical “emanation embodiment” is pluralized under the following inconceivable quantity. Do not silently transfer the second clause’s beings into the first.

[^T01-014]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 421–422, MTP-S000053, MTP-S000054, MTP-S000055, MTP-S000056, MTP-S000057, MTP-S000059, MTP-S000060, MTP-S000061. Tibetan: འཛིན་བྱེད་འཁྲུལ་པའི་འཁོར; ཉོན་མོངས་པ་ལྔ; འཛིན་པ; མི་དགེ་བ. The first expression is provisionally “the retinue of delusion that apprehends,” preserving the apprehending function without silently treating འཛིན་བྱེད as identical to the later canonical འཛིན་པ, “apprehending subject.” “Afflictions” and “nonvirtue” are proposed technical entries. The fixed source supplies only “five afflictions”; names seen in the print are not inserted into this translation. Ignorance, ordinary mind, mental faculty, apprehending subject and conceptual thought keep their distinct canonical assignments. The sentence continues directly across the page-marker pair. Review the first compound and the new terms; the manuscript-comparison apparatus separately reports print-only wording.

[^T01-015]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 422, MTP-S000072. Tibetan: སྒྲ་དང. The canonical standalone assignment is “word.” Here སྒྲ occurs between forms and smells in an enumeration of objects, following seeing, hearing, smelling and tasting functions. “Sounds” is therefore used provisionally for this sensory-object occurrence; it does not change the canonical “word” or the established word-and-meaning compounds. Review this narrow contextual exception and retain its source linkage in any future occurrence.

[^T01-016]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 419, 421–422, MTP-S000017, MTP-S000039, MTP-S000050, MTP-S000062, MTP-S000063, MTP-S000066, MTP-S000067, MTP-S000068, MTP-S000069, MTP-S000071, MTP-S000073, MTP-S000074, MTP-S000075, MTP-S000076. Tibetan: ཡུལ; མཐོང་བྱེད; ཉན་བྱེད; སྣོམ་བྱེད; མྱོན་བྱེད; གཟུགས; དྲི; རོ; རེག་བྱ; ཆོས. “Objects” for ཡུལ is provisional in these relations; the text does not add “apprehended” here, which is reserved to the distinct glossary expression གཟུང་བ. The agentive forms remain “that which sees/hears/smells/tastes,” rather than silently supplying named organs. The bare མྱོན is represented by “tastes” in this list, subject to review of its relation to experiencing. Forms, smells, tastes, tangible objects and phenomena are proposed object-list entries, without silently making ཆོས a doctrine or supplying a mental-organ qualifier. The two English “smells” and “tastes” have different grammatical functions, traceable to the listed exact Tibetan. The meaning of འདུ་བྱེད is separately flagged. Review the faculty/object alignment without adding missing members.

[^T01-017]: Golden-v1, chapter 1, printed 422–423, MTP-S000078, MTP-S000079, MTP-S000080, MTP-S000081, MTP-S000082, MTP-S000083, MTP-S000085, MTP-S000086, MTP-S000087, MTP-S000088, MTP-S000089, MTP-S000091, MTP-S000092, MTP-S000093, MTP-S000094. Tibetan: ལྷ་མ་ཡིན; དྲི་ཟ; བྱང་ཆུབ་སེམས་དཔའ; འདུལ་བ; མདོ་སྡེ; མངོན་པ; ཉན་ཐོས; རང་རྒྱལ; ཀྲི་ཡ; ཨུ་པ; ཡོ་ག; མ་ཧ; ཨ་ནུ; ཨ་ཏི. The unlisted names are provisionally represented as asuras, gandharvas, bodhisattvas, Vinaya, Sūtra, Abhidharma, hearers and solitary victors. The whole expression bodhisattva is not decomposed into the distinct standalone སེམས (“ordinary mind”). Kriyā, Upa, Yoga, Mahā, Anu and Ati represent the supplied short names: no missing “yoga” is appended, no additional vehicle is supplied and no taxonomy is imposed on the mixed assembly list. These conventional target names are not claims that the Tibetan source spells Sanskrit in normalized form. Review as occurrence-linked name/whole-expression proposals, not activated defaults; plurality follows the assembly syntax.

This is an explicit display convention, not a lexical emendation; main words and local source-only content are separately checked below.
